No `log_activity()` (dino/investors precedent).
- **Attachments reuse the `documents` infra** — new `documents.meeting_id` column (a meeting's files = documents where meeting_id=X, stored under `meetings/{id}/…`, project_id/is_company left null). Audio + exhibits upload via the **client-direct signed-upload path** (`/api/documents/signed-upload-url` → `uploadToSignedUrl` → register via JSON `/api/documents`, both extended to honor `meeting_id`) so big recordings bypass the serverless body limit; the bucket's 50MB `file_size_limit` was raised to **500MB** (allowed_mime_types was already NULL/unrestricted on the Studio, so audio was never MIME-blocked there). `documents/[id]` GET/DELETE gained meeting-aware access (`canViewerAccessDoc`).
- **TWO PRE-EXISTING CONSTRAINTS bit meeting-only docs (both fixed in the migration):** (1) `documents_scope_check` required project/entity/company/reference — **widened to also accept `meeting_id is not null`**, else meeting files are rejected. (2) `documents.meeting_id` FK is **ON DELETE CASCADE** (not SET NULL) — a meeting attachment is scoped only by meeting_id, so SET NULL would leave a scopeless row that re-violates the scope check; cascade deletes the files (chunks cascade in turn). The API DELETE route also sweeps the storage objects (a DB cascade can't reach storage).
- **RAG (gated by index_ai):** on save, a self-contained minutes **markdown document** is (re)generated + tagged meeting_id (`src/lib/meetings/document.ts` `syncMeetingDocument`, delete-and-replace snapshot) — doubles as the downloadable/preserved compliance export AND the embedding vehicle (a chunk's document_id satisfies chunks_source_check alone → no chunks-table migration). **Board minutes are is_company=FALSE on the doc row** (kept OUT of the general company KB browse list) but embed as an is_company chunk when opted in; project meetings embed project-scoped. index_ai off → `embedding_status='skipped'`, no chunk.
- **AI-assist:** project MeetingForm has a **"Draft summary from transcript"** button → `POST /api/meetings/draft` runs the session-less `extractMeeting()` (reused from the meeting-intake module) and prefills summary/minutes/attendees/decisions. Board meetings never get AI drafting.
- **Surfaces:** `/company/board` (admin-only by default-deny; new Board tab in `CompanySectionTabs`, gated on the admin `showProfile` flag) + a **Meetings tab on every project** (`ProjectTabBar`, after Updates; project layout already gates canAccessProject — v1 non-admins with project access READ + play recordings, writes admin-only). Both render `MeetingsView` (list → detail/form router; `MeetingForm`/`MeetingDetail`/`MeetingFiles` in `src/components/meetings/`, Panel/Chip/DatePicker/ConfirmDialog idiom; audio plays inline via an on-demand signed URL).
- **Verified:** tsc + eslint + full build clean (local + Studio); migration applied + PostgREST 200 for `meetings` and `documents?meeting_id`; **rolled-back live psql round-trip** — board+project insert, doc link, **cascade delete removes the file**, and both check constraints (scope/project pairing, widened scope-check) enforced; deployed (app 200; `/company/board` 307, `/api/meetings*` 401 unauthed). **NOT yet exercised (eyeball when convenient):** the authenticated end-to-end UI — create a board meeting from the real minutes, upload an actual `.m4a` through the signed-upload flow + play it back, and toggle Include-in-Ber-AI to confirm a project interaction becomes retrievable. **Deliberately deferred:** local Whisper auto-transcription; a top-level cross-portfolio `/meetings` register; opening project-meeting *writes* to project_managers (the `canAccessMeeting`/canAccessProject groundwork is in place — just allowlist `/api/meetings` + the pages when wanted).

**Done 2026-07-28 (Dino Service Pros — internal operating-company revenue tracker; DEPLOYED + MIGRATED):**
- **Context: Richard is acquiring Dino Service Pros (plumbing/HVAC). It's one of OUR companies now — not a vendor, not a project.** Dino does ~$2M/yr, mostly from its own existing clients; the goal is to **track revenue split by SOURCE (internal = Ber Wilson work vs external = Dino's clients)** to show Dino its business increasingly comes from us, plus track the **$150k we owe them over 12 months**. His picks (AskUserQuestion): flexible ledger (itemize internal, lump external OK); **Dino-specific** build (not a generic operating-companies framework); **$150k as an installment schedule**.
- **Placement decision (not a build, done via existing surfaces):** Dino belongs in three places — the **org-structure chart** (`/company/structure`) as a new **division** (Richard adds it — admin Add-division form, no code); the **company capability profile** (`/company` — write self-perform plumbing/HVAC into `capabilities`/`differentiators` + NAICS so Ber AI/`assessFit` factor it in); and the **opportunity** gets marked `closed_won` when the acquisition finalizes (no auto-conversion machinery exists — it's just closed in place). Analysis rejected a steel-clone (steel = quotes/SF-pricing/commissions/own sales role, wrong shape) and investors (money IN, wrong direction) — Dino is a **revenue+relationship tracker**, so it clones the *investors* module shape (money records + rollup band + notes + admin-only + attention wiring).
- **New module `/dino`** (admin-only by default-deny — NOT in any `permissions.ts` allowlist; `/api/dino` routes also carry in-route `getViewer` admin guards). Migration `20260731000001_dino.sql` (**APPLIED to the Studio DB** via ssh+docker psql + PostgREST restarted/reloaded; `database.ts` hand-extended; `DinoRevenue`/`DinoPayment`/`DinoNote` aliases): `dino_revenue` (source_type internal|external, project_id FK→projects **on delete set null**, client_name, amount, revenue_date, description, notes), `dino_payments` (label/amount/due_date/paid/paid_date — the obligation schedule), `dino_notes`. Plain-text source vocab + all math in `src/lib/utils/dino.ts` (`revenueRollup(range: ttm|ytd|all)` → total/internal/external/**internalPct**, `splitByPeriod` monthly/quarterly buckets, payment helpers); no `log_activity()` (investors precedent). Shared write validation in `src/lib/dino/parse.ts`. Payment PATCH is **partial-update** so the mark-paid toggle can't wipe the amount; note author stamped server-side from the session (never client). Deliberately deferred fast-follows: Ber AI/RAG snapshot embedding + agent tool, daily-brief Dino line, generalizing to reusable operating-companies.
- **UI (`src/components/dino/`):** `DinoDashboard` (client orchestrator, range state, `router.refresh()` after mutations) → `RevenueRollup` (band: total / Ber Wilson / external / **Ber Wilson share %** + range selector), `RevenueSplitBar` (pure CSS stacked bars, internal=primary/external=slate — the persuasion picture, no chart lib), `RevenueLedger` (add/edit/delete; internal→project picker, external→client field; DollarHint), `PaymentSchedule` (installments, owed/paid/total, mark-paid toggle, overdue amber), `DinoNotes`. Nav item in `nav.ts` (Wrench icon, primary group). **Attention wiring:** new `dino_payment` category in `computeAttention` (unpaid installments ≤30d/overdue) + dashboard **Needs Attention** "Dino Payments" section + agent `get_attention_items` enum.
- **Verified:** 28-case pure-function unit check of the rollup/split/payment math (**caught + fixed a real bug** — quarter labels rendered "3 '26" not "Q3 '26", `slice(6)`→`slice(5)`); live rolled-back psql round-trip against the Studio (internal row links to a real project, external has client_name/null project, 12 installments=$150k with owed/paid split correct, FK confirmed SET NULL, 0 rows after rollback); PostgREST exposes all 3 tables + resolves the `project:projects(...)` embed the page uses (HTTP 200); tsc + eslint (only the 2 documented dashboard purity survivors) + full build clean; deployed to the Studio (app 200; `/dino` 307 + `/api/dino/*` 401 unauthed). **Eyeball when convenient:** open `/dino` as admin, add an internal (project-linked) + external revenue row and confirm the split band/trend bar, add the $150k installments and confirm one comes up on the dashboard Needs Attention as it nears due. **Manual (Richard):** add the Dino division on `/company/structure`; add self-perform plumbing/HVAC to `/company` capabilities; mark the Dino opportunity `closed_won` when it closes.

- **Richard's ask: sales reps are ONLY Colton/Richard/Eric/Jason; marketing sources can be ANY contact (typically Ber Wilson employees, but outside referrers too); track their referral fees (% of margin OR flat) with editing + tracking.** His AskUserQuestion picks reshaped the marketing model: (1) **unify** — the previously-separate internal tier-rate "Marketer" (5/4/3%) and team-member "Referred By" were **both retired** into ONE **Marketing / Referral Source = any party**, paid a referral fee (flat or % of margin); (2) sales reps managed by a **flag** (add more later in the UI), not a hardcode.
- **Data findings that made this safe:** the internal `marketer_id` tier-rate was used on **0 of 8 deals** and referral fees on **0**; current salespeople were only Eric & Richard (both now flagged reps) — so the reshape had essentially no data to migrate. Colton & Jason didn't exist as team_members and were **created** (role `steel_sales`, no login yet).
- **Migration `20260804000001_steel_marketing_source.sql` (APPLIED to the Studio DB + PostgREST restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended; non-destructive ADD-only):** `team_members.is_steel_rep` (flagged Richard/Eric, inserted Colton/Jason as steel_sales reps); `steel_deals.referral_party_id` (FK→parties **on delete set null**) + index. **Retired but kept (unused, non-destructive):** `steel_deals.marketer_id`, `marketing_rate_override`, `marketing_commission_paid`(+date), and `lead_source_id` (the old team-member referrer). `lead_source`/`lead_source_detail` **stay** as the channel categorization (relabeled "Channel" on the form), distinct from the paid source.
- **Commission math (`steel.ts`):** removed the whole tier-rate marketing path — `DealCommissionInput`/`DealFinancials`/`dealFinancials` dropped `marketing_rate_override`/`hasMarketer`/`marketingAccelerated`/`marketingRate`/`marketingCommission`; deleted `MARKETING_RATE_BY_TIER` + `marketingRate()`. `totalPayout = salesCommission + installFee + referralFee`. **Referral fee unchanged** (`referralFeeAmount(type,value,totalMargin)` — flat or % of the deal's total margin), now paid to `referral_party_id`.
- **Rollups (`rollups.ts`):** `PayoutKind` is now `sales|install|referral` (marketing gone); `payoutItems(rows, members, partyName)` resolves the referral payee from a **party** name map; `RepScorecard` = **sales + install only** (a rep's earnings; the referral fee is a per-source concept, not rep income); new **`referralSourceRollup(rows, partyName)`** → per-source owed/paid/projected/total. `financialsFor` drops marketing.
- **Form (`SteelDealForm`):** Salesperson dropdown = **steel reps only** (edit page unions in the deal's current salesperson if not flagged, so editing never silently drops them; both pages fall back to all active members pre-migration / if none flagged). Removed the Marketer field + marketing rate override. New **"Marketing / Referral Source"** section: an inline **contacts type-ahead** (`SourcePicker`, over ~60 active parties passed to the client, hidden `referral_party_id`) visible to everyone (attribution), with the **referral fee (type + value) financials-only**. `actions.ts` writes `referral_party_id` for any author; fee stays financials-gated. new/edit pages fetch `reps` (`is_steel_rep`) + `contacts` (parties) + the current source for edit prefill.
- **Surfaces updated:** deal detail (referral source resolved from parties; "Marketing / Referral Source" fact; panel props); **`SteelCommissionsPanel`** rewritten (no marketing row; `scope='self'` = a rep's sales+install only, referral is management-only; referral row shows the party name); **`/steel/commissions`** resolves referral party names + renders a new **`ReferralSourceTable`** (referral fees by source); **`/steel/earnings`** now sales+install only; list-page cards resolve the "referred" name from the party; deal PATCH dropped the marketing mark-paid flag.
- **Admin:** `/settings/users` member cards gained a **"Steel rep" toggle** (violet chip) → `PATCH /api/admin/users/[id] {is_steel_rep}` — Richard flags/unflags reps without code.
- **Verified:** tsc + eslint + full `next build` clean; migration applied (2 flagged, Colton+Jason created — the four reps confirmed = Colton/Eric/Jason/Richard); **rolled-back live psql round-trip** (insert with referral_party_id + percent fee, FK join resolves the source name, 0 rows after rollback); deployed (app 200; `/steel*` + `/settings/users` 307, `PATCH /api/admin/users` 401 unauthed). **Eyeball when convenient (authed):** open a steel deal → Edit, confirm the Salesperson list shows only the 4 reps, pick a Marketing/Referral Source from the contact search + set a % referral fee, mark the deal Paid, then check `/steel/commissions` → the referral shows in Outstanding Payouts + the Referral Fees by Source table. Note: an employee who's a marketing source must exist as a **contact** in the directory (the source picker is parties-only, by design).

**Done 2026-08-03 (steel — commission engine aligned to the founding-phase comp plan + per-deal documents; DEPLOYED + MIGRATED):**
- **Context: Richard handed me the CFS commission plan PDF + GTM brief** (`Ber_Wilson_CFS_Commission_Plan.pdf`, `Ber_Wilson_GTM_Brief.pdf` — reps Colton & Jason, $3k/mo media budget, founding-phase ramp). The CRM shipped with a generic "per-line % of margin" model that did NOT encode the actual plan. His AskUserQuestion picks + a follow-up note reshaped the build.
- **The comp plan (now the model):** `Commission = commissionable margin × RATE`, cost basis fixed $20/SF, paid on cash collected. **RATE set by total project SF** (Sales 15/12/10, Marketing 5/4/3 for <20k / 20k-100k / 100k+ SF), applies to the whole deal. **Sales + Marketing are two separate commissionable roles** (one person can hold both). **Installation/frame-assembly is billed separately and earns NO margin commission — flat $250-$500/install job to the salesperson** (Richard's explicit "separate the assembly line from the margin calc"). **Per-rep volume accelerator: +1pt once a rep collects $1M gross profit in a calendar year** (Richard's clarification: per individual rep on their own deals' profit, NOT company-wide as the PDF's original line read).
- **His decisions (AskUserQuestion):** add a first-class **Marketer role** (over reusing the referrer slot); **auto-derive rate from SF tier, editable override** (over manual %); **track install price/cost for P&L but pay fee-only**; and — the reshaping note — **track each rep's total sales / profit / commissions, reps see their own paid/owed/projected, admins mark paid, don't restrict basic users unless important.**
- **Migration `20260803000001_steel_comp_plan.sql` (APPLIED to the Studio DB via ssh+docker psql + PostgREST restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended, non-destructive ADD-only):** `steel_deals` gained `marketer_id` (FK→team_members on delete set null), `sales_rate_override`/`marketing_rate_override` (null = tier rate), `install_fee` (+`install_fee_paid`/date), deal-level `sales_commission_paid`/`marketing_commission_paid` (+dates), and `collected_date` (stamped when a deal reaches Paid — the accelerator's calendar-year basis). `documents` gained `steel_deal_id` (on delete cascade) + widened `documents_scope_check` to accept it (mirrors the meetings pattern). **Per-line `commission_pct`/`commission_paid` on `steel_deal_services` are now VESTIGIAL** (rate is deal-level) — left in place, unused; the `commissionable` flag stays (freight/permit exclusion).
- **Commission math rewritten in `src/lib/utils/steel.ts`:** `steelSizeTier`/`SIZE_TIER_LABELS`, `SALES_RATE_BY_TIER`/`MARKETING_RATE_BY_TIER`, `salesRate()`/`marketingRate()` (tier base → override wins → +1 if accelerated), `commissionableMargin()` (**excludes installation/assembly-category lines AND non-commissionable lines**), `installMargin()`, `isInstallCategory()`, `ACCELERATOR_THRESHOLD=$1M`, `INSTALL_FEE_MIN/MAX`, and a new `dealFinancials(lines, DealCommissionInput)` returning revenue/cost/margin/commissionableMargin/installMargin/salesRate/marketingRate/salesCommission/marketingCommission/installFee/referralFee/totalPayout/net. **Removed** `serviceCommission` + `DEFAULT_SERVICE_COMMISSION_PCT` (per-line commission is gone).
- **New `src/lib/steel/rollups.ts`** — single source of truth for per-rep scorecards + the accelerator + the payout worklist (pure functions, shared by the commissions page and the rep earnings page): `groupServices`, `acceleratorMap` (two-pass: collected profit is rate-independent → sets the flag → commissions computed with it), `financialsFor`, `repScorecards` (sales/profit/owed/paid/projected per role + accelerator YTD), `payoutItems` (deal-level: sales/marketing/install/referral, each backed by its own paid flag via `PAID_FIELD`/`PAYOUT_LABELS`).
- **Confidentiality shift (was: reps see zero financials):** management (admin/exec, `canSeeSteelFinancials`) sees the whole deal + company rollups + marks paid. **A rep (steel_sales) now sees their OWN cut** — the deal-detail panel has a `scope='self'` mode showing only the roles they hold, and a new **`/steel/earnings`** page (their sales/profit/commissions owed-paid-projected by role + accelerator progress bar + their deals). `/steel/commissions` (management) rewritten: headline band + payout worklist + **`RepScorecardTable`** (all reps). `/steel/commissions` now redirects a non-financials rep to `/steel/earnings`. "My Earnings" link added to the /steel toolbar for everyone; "Commissions" stays financials-only.
- **Deal form (`SteelDealForm`) rewritten:** added Marketer picker, a "Commission Rates & Install" section (tier display + sales/marketing rate overrides + install fee, financials-only) with live readback, and dropped the per-line Commission % input (assembly lines show a "flat fee, not margin-commissioned" note). `actions.ts parseFields`: marketer_id set by any author (attribution); rate overrides + install fee are financials-only (absent-key preservation for reps). Deal PATCH route stamps `collected_date` on Paid and gained financials-gated mark-paid for all four deal-level payouts.
- **Per-deal documents:** new `SteelDealFiles` component + a dedicated **`/api/steel/documents*`** API (signed-upload-url / register / GET-signed-url / DELETE) kept inside the already-permitted `/api/steel` prefix (cleaner than widening the global `/api/documents` for steel_sales). Any steel worker can attach plans/quotes/signed orders to any deal (module shares all deals); files live under `steel-deals/<id>/`, no AI embedding. Removed the now-dead `/api/steel/services/[id]` per-line paid route.
- **Verified:** 22-case pure-math unit check incl. the comp plan's exact worked example (15,000 SF @ $32/SF → **$27,000**); **live end-to-end round-trip against the Studio DB** (13/13: new-column insert, commissionable margin excludes install+freight, sales 12%/marketing 4%/install flat/referral 5%-of-total-margin, 4-item payout worklist, rep scorecard owed, `documents.steel_deal_id` insert under the widened scope check, **cascade delete → 0 orphan services + 0 orphan documents**); tsc + eslint + full build clean; deployed (app 200; `/steel/earnings` + `/steel/commissions` 307, `/api/steel/documents*` 401 unauthed). **Eyeball when convenient (authed):** create a deal with materials+engineering+assembly lines + a marketer + an install fee, mark it Paid, confirm the deal panel + `/steel/commissions` scorecards, mark a payout paid, then log in as a steel_sales rep and confirm `/steel/earnings` shows only their cut.

**Done 2026-08-03 (steel — rep workspace redesign: Kanban pipeline, My/All scope, needs-attention, tab bar; DEPLOYED, no migration):**
- **Context: granting Colton & Jason (steel_sales reps) access; Richard asked what would make the module's UX/UI best for reps managing pipelines + marketing.** The plumbing was excellent but the layout was exec-oriented (a card grid sorted by updated_at, scattered toolbar links). His AskUserQuestion pick: **Full workspace redesign.** Presentation-layer only — reuses the existing stage PATCH, `repScorecards`, overdue helper, `canWorkSteel`; NO schema/API change.
- **Kanban pipeline board (`SteelPipelineBoard.tsx`, new):** replaces the `SteelDealsClient` card grid on `/steel` as the default. Columns = the full funnel (`STEEL_PIPELINE` = quote→…→paid) + a muted **Lost** off-ramp column; each header shows count + Σ value; cards sorted soonest-next-step then value. **Drag a card to another column → advances its stage** (native HTML5 DnD, mirrors `ObjectivesBoard`; optimistic + revert on failure via the same `PATCH /api/steel/deals/[id] {stage}` the detail dropdown uses; `router.refresh()` after). Compact `PipelineCard` (name/customer/value/sqft/source chip/below-floor flag/salesperson avatar in All scope/next-step overdue amber). **Board | List** segmented toggle (List reuses `SteelDealsClient`); persisted via `useStoredState('bw.steel.view')`. Horizontal-scroll on mobile; DnD is desktop-only (touch → open the deal to change stage, noted in the UI).
- **My Pipeline vs All Deals:** `useStoredState('bw.steel.scope')`, **defaults to "mine"** when the viewer has a linked `teamMemberId` (scoped to deals they're the salesperson on); toggle to All (shared pipeline decision unchanged — it's a default view, not a restriction). All-scope adds a salesperson filter; both scopes keep a lead-source filter. Filters are now **client-side/in-board** (the old URL `?stage/source/sales` params + `SteelFilters.tsx` are gone — `SteelFilters` deleted).

**Done 2026-07-26 (steel — commissions owed-logic bug fix + custom line items; DEPLOYED + MIGRATED):**
- **Richard's report: a commission showed as "needs to be paid" on the IHC Steel deal even at QUOTE stage.** Root cause: `/steel/commissions` built the payout worklist from every non-lost deal, so any deal with service prices entered produced an "owed" commission the moment prices existed — the model had **no notion of when a commission is EARNED**. Fix + a line-items redesign, both his AskUserQuestion picks: payable trigger = **cash collected (Paid stage)**; **fix bug now, then custom line items**.
- **Pass 1 (bug fix, no migration):** new `isCommissionPayable(stage)` in `steel.ts` (=== `'paid'`, the SINGLE trigger point — change here to pay at invoice instead). `/steel/commissions` now only counts **collected** deals in the payout worklist + owed/paid rollups + by-salesperson/by-referrer; **open deals surface in a new "Projected — open deals" (forecast, dashed) section** (per-salesperson list, never a payable). Headline "Net after comm." still subtracts all commitments across active deals (fully-loaded net if everything closes). Deal-detail `SteelCommissionsPanel` status pill shows **"Projected"** (neutral) until the deal is collected, then Owed/Paid. IHC at quote now shows Projected, not owed.
- **Pass 2 (custom line items — migration `20260730000001_steel_line_items.sql`, APPLIED to Studio DB + PostgREST restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended):** the fixed 3 services (materials/engineering/assembly) became a list of **custom LINE ITEMS**. `steel_deal_services` gained `description` (free text, falls back to category label) + `commissionable` (bool, the "eligible margin" toggle — pass-through costs like freight untick it → earn no commission); **dropped the `unique(deal_id, service_type)` constraint** so a deal can have multiple lines per category. `service_type` is now the CATEGORY (materials|engineering|assembly|**other**). Existing 3-service deals migrate as-is. `serviceCommission()` returns 0 when `commissionable === false`. New helpers `steelCategory()`, `lineItemLabel()`.
  - **Form (`SteelDealForm`):** dynamic add/remove line rows (description + category dropdown + price, plus cost/commission-%/commissionable checkbox for financials users); the first **materials** line's price still auto-fills from SF×$/SF and its cost from SF×$20 until edited; floor check = sum of materials-line prices ÷ SF. FormData contract: `line_count` + per-line `line_{i}_{id,description,category,price,cost,commission_pct,commissionable}`.
  - **`saveServices()` rewritten (`actions.ts`):** reconciles by row **id** (upsert existing on PK / insert new / delete removed), not by service_type. Non-financials save still preserves cost/commissionable/commission_pct/commission_paid from the existing row (a sales user can't wipe or see them); empty lines dropped.
  - **Detail panel + commissions rollups** render the line description (+ category sublabel); non-commissionable lines show **n/c** and $0 commission. "By service" rollup keys on category (now includes Other).
- **Verified:** tsc + eslint + full build clean (both passes); live round-trip against the Studio DB — two same-category `materials` rows insert (constraint gone), non-commissionable freight line → $0 commission, math correct ($245k rev → $90k margin → $9k commission → $81k net), cascade delete leaves 0 orphans; both deployed (health 200). **Eyeball when convenient:** create a deal with a few line items as admin (incl. one non-commissionable), confirm the projected-vs-owed split on /steel/commissions once it's marked Paid, and that a steel_sales session sees description/category/price but no cost/commission.

**Done 2026-07-25 (steel commissions & margin engine — service line items, per-service commission, referral fees, paid tracking, report; DEPLOYED + MIGRATED):**
- **Richard's ask: track commissions + referral fees on steel deals and report them.** His business: a deal is composed of commissionable **services** — **Steel/Materials, Engineering** (markup over the engineer's fee), **Frame Assembly** — each with **price − cost = margin**; the **salesperson earns a % of each service's margin** (always), and the **lead-source referrer earns a flat fee OR % of total margin**. Steel scope ends at **frame assembly** (full home builds live in the construction module). His picks (via AskUserQuestion): **fixed service sections** (not open line items), **per-service commission %**, **track paid vs unpaid**.
- **Model:** new **`steel_deal_services`** table (migration `20260727000001_steel_commissions.sql`, **APPLIED to the Studio DB** + PostgREST restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended, `SteelDealService` alias): deal_id (cascade), service_type (materials|engineering|assembly), price, cost, commission_pct (salesperson % of THAT service's margin), commission_paid + commission_paid_date, `unique(deal_id, service_type)`. **`steel_deals`** gained referral fields: referral_fee_type (none|flat|percent-of-margin), referral_fee_value, referral_fee_paid + date. **Deal `value` is now COMPUTED = Σ service prices** (the old standalone Contract Value input is gone; sqft×$/SF now just *suggests* the materials price). New stage **`assembled` (Frame Assembly)** between delivered and paid (list backlog now includes it). All math in `src/lib/utils/steel.ts` (`serviceMargin`/`serviceCommission`/`referralFeeAmount`/`dealFinancials`, `DEFAULT_SERVICE_COMMISSION_PCT=10`).
- **CONFIDENTIALITY (my call, Richard didn't object): cost/margin/commission/referral + the report are admin/executive-only.** New `canSeeSteelFinancials(viewer) = admin||executive` in viewer.ts. steel_sales users enter deals + **prices** (revenue) but never see cost/commission. `SteelDealForm` gained `canSeeFinancials` + `services` props: three fixed service sections, cost/commission/referral inputs rendered only for financials users; live margin/commission/referral readback. **Server-side preservation:** `saveServices()` in `actions.ts` — a non-financials save updates only prices and **preserves existing cost/commission** (a sales user can't wipe them); commission_paid/date always preserved across a normal save; upsert onConflict(deal_id,service_type), empty services deleted.
- **Detail page:** `SteelCommissionsPanel` (client, financials-only) — services table (price/cost/margin/comm%/commission + **Mark paid** toggle per service), referral row, totals + net-after-commissions. **Mark-paid APIs:** `PATCH /api/steel/services/[id]` {paid} (new route, `canSeeSteelFinancials` guard) + `referral_fee_paid` branch added to the deal PATCH (financials-gated — `/api/steel` is allowlisted for steel_sales so both carry in-route guards).
- **Report: `/steel/commissions`** (linked from the /steel toolbar, financials-only; page redirects steel_sales→/steel since the `/steel` prefix would otherwise admit them): headline band (revenue/cost/margin/net), **owed vs paid** for salesperson commissions + referral fees, rollups **by salesperson**, **by referrer**, **by service**. Lost deals excluded.
- **Verified:** commission math checked by hand against the $350k example ($95k margin → $11,250 salesperson + $4,750 referral → $79k net); upsert-onConflict collapses to one row per service + cascade-delete leaves 0 orphans (live rolled-back psql test on the Studio); tsc + eslint (fixed a `static-components` error by hoisting PaidToggle) + full build clean; deployed (health 200). **Authed end-to-end UI click-through not yet done** — worth an eyeball: create a deal with all three services as admin, mark a commission paid, check the report; and confirm a steel_sales session sees prices but no cost/commission and no Commissions link.

**Done 2026-07-25 (add users without invite + delete deactivated users; steel deals get a payable referrer; DEPLOYED + MIGRATED):**

A login-less member's card gains a **"Grant access"** button (PATCH now creates + links the auth account when `auth_user_id` is null; still resets password when it exists). `POST /api/admin/users` gained `create_login`/`password`; `PATCH /api/admin/users/[id]` gained the grant-access branch + optional `email`.
- **Ask 2: permanently delete deactivated users** (declutter the admin screen). New **`DELETE /api/admin/users/[id]`** — guarded: member must be **inactive first** (deactivate → then Delete, red button + ConfirmDialog), can't delete self. Removes the auth login (`admin.auth.admin.deleteUser`, non-fatal) then the row. Safe because every FK to `team_members` is `ON DELETE SET NULL` (tasks.assignee_id/waiting_on_id, steel_deals.salesperson_id + new lead_source_id, objectives.owner_id, investors.relationship_owner_id) or CASCADE (access_grants) — no orphans, no blocks.

Lead source stays the free-text self-maintaining category; new **"Referred By"** dropdown on the deal form links the specific person to a real user via **`steel_deals.lead_source_id` → team_members** (migration `20260726000002_steel_lead_source_referrer.sql`, **APPLIED to the Studio DB** + PostgREST restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended; index + FK `on delete set null`). Nullable (channels like Trade Show have no referrer). Salesperson dropdown was already every active `team_member`, so an Add-User (even login-less) shows up as a salesperson automatically. Detail page shows a "Referred By" fact.
- **Commission tracking is the DELIBERATE NEXT PIECE (Richard flagged it, said "later"; NOT built).** Plan told to him: per deal, two payees — salesperson (**always % of profit**) and lead-source referrer (**flat fee OR % of profit** for marketing). Two gaps beyond the now-shipped payable-identity link: (1) **commission terms per payee** (rate/type/amount), and (2) **a profit/cost figure on the deal** — `steel_deals.value` is contract REVENUE, not profit, so "% of profit" has no basis yet (add a cost or profit column). Build these together as one focused pass when he's ready; the `salesperson_id` + `lead_source_id` links are the forward-compatible foundation.

**Done 2026-07-24 (Ber AI reads whole documents + iPhone chat fix; DEPLOYED, data repaired):**
- **Richard's report: asking Ber AI to "review all the documents on this project" returned only snippets.** Two real gaps, both fixed: (1) **there was no tool to list a project's documents** — "review everything uploaded" had no supported path; new **`list_documents`** agent tool (project scope from args or conversation context, `scope='company'` for the KB, `include_children` for programs; returns id/file_name/doc_type/ai_summary/`has_full_text` without ever pulling the text, + a hint to chain into get_document_content). (2) **`search_knowledge_base` hid `document_id`** — match_chunks always returned it but the tool layer dropped it, so the agent could never follow its own instruction to fetch full text after a snippet hit; results now carry `document_id` + a "these are snippets, fetch the full document" note. Agent prompt gains a **Reviewing documents** rule (list → get_document_content, several per turn → answer from full text; snippets are pointers). Verified live against the Studio DB: list on a real 11-doc project, company scope, no-scope error, get_document_content chain, and all 8 KB results carrying document_id.
- **Data repair while testing: 5 project docx files had NO stored text** (4 `skipped` pre-docx-support uploads incl. the CRISI decision brief + StracNet submission, 1 `complete`-but-empty) — all re-run through `runDocumentAiPass` standalone (chunks wiped per doc first); **0 documents remain without extracted text platform-wide.**
- **iPhone: the chat send button bled off the right edge.** Cause: iOS Safari auto-zooms when focusing any input under 16px (the chat textarea was 14.5px), pushing fixed-width controls off the visual viewport. Fixed: viewport **`maximumScale: 1`** in layout.tsx (kills focus auto-zoom app-wide; iOS still allows pinch), chat textarea `text-base sm:text-sm` + `min-w-0`, messages area `overflow-x-hidden`, bubbles `break-words` (long URLs/tables can't force horizontal overflow).
- **Also committed + shipped: the prior session's in-flight work** found uncommitted in the tree (verified by tsc/lint/build): conversation delete (owner-checked GET/DELETE on `/api/ai/agent`, ConversationList row delete, AgentChat clear control on the intake document reader), task-board **Manage Team dialog** (add/remove members, open-task reassignment; team-members POST dedupes by active name; new `/api/team-members/[id]`), corrected header Drive link.

Fixed both ends: (1) **10s heartbeat** (`{type:'ping'}`) from the agent route's SSE branch while runAgent works (client ignores unknown event types); (2) **client recovery in AgentChat** — the server keeps running and persists the answer after a drop (it always did), so on a network-layer stream failure the chat now polls the conversation (5s interval, 4 min cap; fresh chats re-found by scope + title) and renders the persisted answer instead of erroring; server-reported failures carry a `noRecover` marker and surface immediately. **Scoping architecture confirmed correct as-is**: dock scopes to the project from the URL, no scope = portfolio-wide. **Verified against PRODUCTION with a real minted session** (GoTrue admin generateLink → verifyOtp → `@supabase/ssr`-serialized cookies — a reusable pattern for authed prod API tests): project-scoped ask autonomously called the new `list_documents` tool and streamed the doc list (max silent gap 6.7s, pings at 10s/20s); portfolio ask used `list_projects` (gap 6.1s); both test conversations deleted through the live DELETE endpoint (200).

- **Context: Richard's verdict was the platform didn't look polished; approved direction = visuals + structural simplification, refined evolution of the navy identity (not a rebrand).** Full audit first (Explore agent + hand verification), then 4 phases.

Fixed (`--font-sans: var(--font-geist-sans), ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif`); verified `font-family:var(--font-geist-sans),…` in built CSS. **Dark mode neutrals now carry the same hue-260 cool tint as light** (was pure gray — the two themes finally share a temperature).
- **One design language, enforced by primitives:** `ui/card.tsx` rewritten as **`Panel`/`PanelHeader`** (the REAL app idiom `rounded-xl border-border bg-card elev-1`; old shadcn Card had zero importers); new **`ui/chip.tsx`** (`<Chip tone={*_BADGE[...]}>`) — the one badge shell, consuming the constants.ts color maps (which gained the 4 missing dark-variant sets: pwinBadge, BID_DECISION, ENTITY_CATEGORY, FEDERAL_STANDARD); dead `ui/badge.tsx` deleted; new **`@utility label-caps`** in globals.css = the canonical 11px small-caps label (replaced ~120 drifting spellings app-wide — text-[10px]/[11px]/text-xs × wide/wider/widest all normalized); **raw `shadow-sm/md/lg/xl` swept to `.elev-*`** (~26 files; map marker internals + variant-prefixed forms excluded); `tabular-nums` → `.tnum` everywhere; raw `text-blue-600` links → `text-primary`. Decorative CSS deleted: `.bg-depth` radial gradients, `.divider-fade`, `.stagger-children`, per-band inline animationDelays (one page-level fade remains). **Rules for new UI: Panel/Chip/label-caps, `.elev-*` never `shadow-*`, nested sub-blocks are `rounded-md bg-muted/30` (no border/shadow).**
- **Nav has ONE source of truth: `src/lib/nav.ts`** (items + groups + titles + palette keywords + `alsoMatches` highlighting) consumed by AppSidebar, AppHeader (titles), MobileNav (primary = `mobilePrimary` flag, More = the rest), CommandPalette. Role gating stays per-consumer (`canAccessPage` at render time). The old 4 divergent copies are gone.
- **Sidebar 17 → 11 + gear (Richard's picks):** (1) **`/intake`** = one Intake destination with Email | Proposal tabs (`?tab=` idiom); `/email-ingestion` + `/proposals/intake` are redirect() stubs (the `/email-ingestion/[id]` review routes stay, breadcrumbed to /intake); admin-only by default-deny like both old routes. (2) **Calendar folded under Intel** — shared `IntelSectionTabs` (Ask Ber AI | Calendar) on both pages, `/calendar` route kept for deep links, nav entry removed. (3) **System group (Review Queue/Activity/Users/Health) tucked behind a gear** at the sidebar bottom — expands in place, auto-expands when on a system page, amber dot when review queue is non-empty; still in mobile More + ⌘K.
- **Dashboard morning read decluttered (5 full-width bands → 3):** **AlertsBanner deleted** — it rebuilt the same dd/milestone/cert data the NeedsAttention rail already shows; unique content (mailbox-broken alert, expiring certs) moved into a red **Critical section at the top of NeedsAttention** (props `mailboxAlert`/`expiringCerts`). **VerticalRollup moved to the right rail** as a compact list (Chip + count + value). **DailyBrief is collapsed by default and NEVER auto-generates** (local model = 30–60s; cached brief renders instantly, fresh one is an explicit Generate/refresh click; expansion persisted via `useStoredState('bw.brief.expanded')`). Stack now: Now objectives → KPI band → brief line → projects + rail.
- **ProjectCard calmed (7 info rows → ~4):** stage text badge dropped (stage = left border color + StageIndicator bar, told once not thrice); top row = sector Chip (+status if ≠active, +Program) with the bid-due date inline right (red ≤7d, amber ≤21d, muted beyond); pwin is muted text under the value, not a third badge; counts + deadline + blockers merged into one meta line; flat p-4. Projects list: ProgramBanner violet one-off → neutral card idiom. Project detail header → Chips. Tasks board: one `fieldClass`/`filterFieldClass` (h-8 rhythm), workload chips' nested pills → colored text, buttons h-8.
- Deliberately left: print-page letterheads (`tracking-[0.15em]` slate — PDF-tuned document styling), chip-style uppercase severity badges, map marker internals, AVATAR_CLASSES (distinct role).
- **Verified:** tsc + eslint (20 = the documented survivors, unchanged) + full build clean; prod `next start` boundary smoke (login 200, pages 307, API 401); dev renders against the Studio DB (dashboard bands, intake tabs both ways, intel↔calendar cross-tabs); per-role nav composition checked against real `canAccessPage` (admin 15, executive 2, PM 3, member 1 — identical to before); Geist + label-caps + tinted dark palette confirmed in built CSS; old intake routes 307.

**Done 2026-07-16 (company KB open/download + delete removes indexed data; DEPLOYED):**
- **Richard's report: company-profile Knowledge Base files couldn't be clicked open/downloaded, and he wanted certainty that deleting a file removes its indexed data (no stale proposals tainting RAG).** Both were real bugs.
- **Open/download:** `CompanyKnowledgeBase.tsx` was missed in the 07-08 signed-URL pass — filenames rendered as plain `<p>`, no view/download wiring at all (the server route `GET /api/documents/[id]` already worked). Now: filename click → `viewDocument` (inline for PDF/image/text, download fallback for docx), plus a Download icon button; `mime_type` added to the company page select + `CompanyDoc`.
- **Delete was broken for EVERY indexed document, app-wide:** `chunks.document_id` FK was `ON DELETE SET NULL`, and the cascaded SET NULL violates `chunks_source_check` (needs ≥1 source id) → the DB delete errored → "Delete failed" toast. Silver lining: no orphaned chunks could ever exist (verified zero), the deletes just failed. Migration `20260716000001_chunks_delete_cascade.sql` (**APPLIED to the Studio DB**, supabase-rest restarted) flips `document_id` AND `update_id` FKs to `ON DELETE CASCADE` (every other chunk source FK already cascaded) — **deleting a document/update now provably removes its chunks** (verified in a rolled-back live transaction: delete succeeds, 0 orphans). `extracted_text`/`ai_summary` live on the row itself, so they die with it too.
- **Stranding hazard fixed in both delete routes** (`api/documents/[id]`, `api/opportunities/documents/[id]`): they removed the storage object BEFORE the DB row, so a failed row delete (which was happening every time, see above) would have left a row pointing at a missing file. Order flipped: DB row first (cascades chunks), storage cleanup after, non-fatal. Verified zero already-stranded rows in prod.
- `tsc` + eslint + full build clean; deployed (health 200, doc route 401s unauthed, /company 307s).

**Done 2026-07-13 (task handoffs + printable weekly report; DEPLOYED):**
- **Context: Richard's team can't reach the tailnet-only platform, so he routes everything by hand.** Two pains: the team can't see priority, and he's the human router for "Eric owes Dennis the survey before Nancy can file". Decision (his picks): a **blocker on the task** (not task→task dependencies, not free text) + a **print-to-PDF weekly report he emails himself** (not a public share link — that would mean re-exposing the Studio, with the token as the only boundary in front of a service-role app; and not platform-sent email yet — revisit once the format has settled).
- **Tasks gain a waiting-on triple** (migration `20260713000001_task_handoffs.sql`, **APPLIED to the Studio DB**, supabase-rest restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended): `waiting_on_id` (FK→team_members), `waiting_on_what`, `waiting_on_since`. Written and cleared **as a triple** — shared logic in `src/lib/tasks/handoff.ts` (`resolveWaitingOn` — setting a blocker requires saying what they owe, `since` defaults to today; `selfBlockError` — checked against the *effective* post-patch values so it catches both "block it on its own assignee" and "reassign it to the person it's already waiting on"). Wired into tasks POST/PATCH (400 on a self-block) + GET filters `?blocked=1` / `?blocking=<member>`. UI: Waiting-on row in `TaskDetailSheet` (person + what, aged, Clear button), amber "waiting on {name} · {n}d" chip on board cards (stale ≥7d), a **Blocked** filter, and a second count on the per-person workload chips — **holding up N** — which is the follow-up list.
- **`/reports/weekly/print`** — chromeless print-to-PDF document (clones the `/objectives/print` pattern; no PDF library). Organized **objective → person → handoff**, which makes priority mechanical (anything under a Now objective) instead of a weekly judgment call. Five sections: Now/Soon objectives w/ health + task counts, **Handoffs** (`{blocker} → {assignee}  {what}  {age}`, oldest first), Each person's week (overdue / due this week / **owes others** / waiting on others / no date), Dates that move (milestones + bid dates ≤14d), Closed last week. **`?person=<id>` narrows it to one teammate's page — that's the artifact that actually gets emailed.** Admin-only by default-deny (`/reports` is in no `permissions.ts` allowlist; link from the /tasks toolbar is gated on `viewer.isAdmin`). `layout.tsx`'s shell exclusion is now `pathname.endsWith('/print')` (was the hardcoded objectives path).
- **Fixed while here: `task_notes.author` was never populated.** The column existed and the API accepted it, but `TaskDetailSheet` posted only `{ body }` — every note in the system was unattributed. It's now stamped server-side from `getViewer()`, **never** taken from the client (otherwise a note could be posted under someone else's name).
- **GOTCHA (cost a whole class of runtime breakage): adding a second FK from `tasks` to `team_members` made every existing `assignee:team_members(...)` embed AMBIGUOUS** — PostgREST returns `PGRST201`, `tsc` catches only one of them. Every task surface (board, /tasks page, project tasks tab, opportunity + investor detail, agent `query_investor`) would have 500'd. All 7 call sites now hint the constraint: `assignee:team_members!tasks_assignee_id_fkey(...)`. **Rule: adding a second FK to an already-embedded table breaks every existing embed of it — hint the constraint name in all of them.**
- **Verified:** handoff helpers unit-tested (9 cases); live against the Studio stack — both embeds resolve, the un-hinted form confirmed to fail with PGRST201, insert/join/`?blocked`/`?blocking`/clear round-trip, test rows deleted; report rendered against real data with seeded handoffs (all 5 sections, correct ages + sort, chromeless with the middleware `x-pathname` header, `?person=` scoping showing both directions of a handoff); board HTML shows chip + Blocked filter + "holding up N". `tsc` + eslint (20 = the documented pre-existing survivors) + full build clean; deployed; live route 307s unauthenticated (the report is NOT publicly readable). All test data removed.
- **Note for Richard:** "Dates that move" will render empty until there's data — the `milestones` table is **empty** and **no project has a `bid_due_date`**. Deliberately deferred: an AI-written narrative on the report (the daily-brief cron already assembles this data and writes markdown to `stored_briefs` that **nothing renders** — there's no `/briefs` page; layer it in once the factual format has proven itself) and platform-sent email (would need `Mail.Send` on the Graph scopes + one Azure re-consent).

**Done 2026-07-12 (document indexing: docx support, honest statuses, reindex button; DEPLOYED):**
- **Richard's report: company Knowledge Base docs stuck on "Indexing…" forever, one PDF failed.** Root cause was NOT the Studio sleeping: **the doc AI pass only handled PDFs + plain text — every `.docx` was silently skipped** and sat at the default `embedding_status='pending'` (which the UI renders as "Indexing…"). 7 of 11 company docs were docx (investor package, FAQ, exec one-pager, brag sheet) with zero AI coverage. The failed PDF (`BerWilson_WalkAway_PlatformEdition.pdf`, uploaded 07-10) was the pre-fix `maxTokens:512` empty-summary bug (verified in `ai_queries`: tokens_out=512, empty response, `ai_summary=''`).
- **Word (.docx) is now a first-class indexed type**: new dep `mammoth` → `extractDocxText` in `document-text.ts` (≥40 chars or null). Legacy binary `.doc` remains unsupported → status `skipped` ("Not indexed" chip), file still stored.
- **New shared `src/lib/ai/document-pipeline.ts`** — `runDocumentAiPass()` (never throws; settles `embedding_status` to complete/error/skipped) + `documentKind()` (mime + extension fallback). Replaced the three near-copies: `api/documents/upload`, `api/documents` (both now fully awaited — no more fire-and-forget embed, so a mid-embed restart can't strand 'processing'), and email-intake `processPromotedDocumentAi` (documents-table branch uses the pipeline; opportunity_documents branch + `api/opportunities/documents` gained docx via `documentKind`/`extractDocxText` by hand — that table has no status column). `embedDocument` now returns boolean. Uploads with `extract_ai=false` set `skipped` instead of dangling at 'pending'.
- **Reindex without re-upload**: `POST /api/documents/[id]/reindex` (maxDuration 300) — downloads from storage, **deletes the doc's old chunks** (no double-index), re-runs the pipeline. Same viewer guard as the [id] GET/DELETE (non-admin: granted projects only; company docs admin-only). Company KB rows that aren't `complete` show a RefreshCw button; `EmbedStatus` gained the muted `skipped` → "Not indexed" state (tooltip explains). Upload hint copy now says "Word (.docx)".
- **All 8 stuck company docs re-indexed live** via repo lib against the Studio stack (script pattern: alias-loader + `--env-file`); verified statuses flipped to complete with real summaries + chunks. `tsc` + eslint + full build clean; deployed to the Studio.

**Done 2026-07-12 (email intake: discard reviewed packages + default-to-project; DEPLOYED):**
- **Richard's asks: (a) a way to delete a reviewed ingestion he doesn't want, (b) intake should default to creating a project — opportunity only for buying/acquiring a company — because switching kinds lost the prepopulated data.**
- **Discard is now first-class.** The existing PATCH dismiss endpoint already allowed dismissing pending sessions (it clears staged attachment files too), but the UI only showed the ✕ on failed rows. Now: pending rows in the Recent list get the same dismiss ✕, and the review screen (`EmailIngestReview`) gains a **Discard** button (ConfirmDialog) that dismisses and returns to `/email-ingestion`. Confirmed sessions stay undismissable by design — they're the record of what was created.
- **Project is the default kind.** Prompt (`email-intake-1.2`): "project" is stated as the default; "opportunity" ONLY for corporate transactions (purchase/acquisition of a company, merger, divestiture, equity investment in a business); partnerships/JVs/teaming around built work = project. Code fallback flipped to match: `normalize()` in `analyze.ts` now defaults `suggested_record` to `project` when the model omits/garbles it (was opportunity).
- **Switching kinds no longer loses data.** The review toggle now goes through `switchKind()`, which carries shared facts into the other record's blanks (name, sector, location, value; description↔objective/thesis; client↔counterparty/target) — never overwriting user edits. This works regardless of how completely the model filled both objects.
- `tsc` + eslint + full build clean; deployed to the Studio.

**Done 2026-07-11 (email intake: attachment picker + research-report document; DEPLOYED):**
- **Richard's ask: keep the prepopulated record creation, but also (a) get a document that fully summarizes the discussions + attachments, and (b) pull the attachments themselves and pick which to upload onto the created project/opportunity.** Both shipped; the run/review/confirm architecture absorbed it as an extension, no rework.
- **Attachments are now staged during the research run.** `/api/email-research/run` uploads every non-inline attachment (ALL file types incl. docx/xlsx — wider than the AI-analysis set; ≤25MB each, 30/run, deduped by name+size across threads) to storage at `email-intake/{sessionId}/…` and records `StagedAttachment[]` on new column **`email_intake_sessions.staged_attachments`** (migration `20260711000002`, **APPLIED to the Studio DB**, supabase-rest restarted; `database.ts` hand-extended). The 3-per-thread AI-analysis cap stays (local Qwen is 30–60s/attachment); report lines now say "kept for review" instead of just "skipped". Failure paths clean the staging files; dismiss (PATCH) does too.
- **Review screen gains an Attachments section** (`EmailIngestReview`): checkbox per file (default all checked), size + source thread + "content in report" marker, View button via new `GET /api/email-ingestion/sessions/[id]/attachment?path=…` (server-side signed URL, same pattern as document-links; validates the path belongs to the session; admin-only by default like the rest of email-ingestion).
- **Confirm promotes the picks + attaches the report.** `confirm` accepts `attachment_paths`, copies selected files into `projects/{id}/…` or `opportunities/{id}/…`, inserts `documents`/`opportunity_documents` rows, then runs the standard AI pass (summary + extracted_text + embedding) **fire-and-forget sequentially after the response** (`src/lib/email-ingestion/attachments.ts` — `promoteStagedAttachment`/`processPromotedDocumentAi`). It also saves the **full research report as a named .md document** on the record ("Email research — {label}.md"), headed by a new AI-written **`discussion_summary`** (multi-paragraph narrative; `email-intake-1.1` prompt) with the full transcript below; `extracted_text` stored so the agent can quote it, deliberately NOT re-embedded (the update-row / embedOpportunityReport paths already index that content). Staging folder cleared after promotion; `created_record_ids` gains `document_ids`.
- **Found + fixed while testing (both pre-existing):** (1) **`maxTokens: 512` on doc summaries returns an EMPTY summary in local mode** — Qwen burns the whole budget on reasoning (`tokens_out: 512`, empty response verified in `ai_queries`). Bumped to 2048 in the new lib AND both existing upload routes (`api/documents`, `api/opportunities/documents`). (2) **Deleting a project failed platform-wide** (`activity_log_project_id_fkey` violation): cascade-deleted children (documents/updates/tasks…) fire their log triggers after the project row is gone and the audit INSERT references the dead project. Migration `20260711000003_activity_log_cascade_delete_fix.sql` (**APPLIED**) retries the insert with `project_id` NULL on FK violation — audit row kept (metadata still snapshots the old row). Verified live: project delete now succeeds, children cascade, audit rows survive (historical rows get project_id nulled by the FK's existing SET NULL, as before).
- **Verified live end-to-end against the Studio stack** (staged session + files via repo lib, real confirm POST through a dev server): report doc (markdown, discussion summary + ai_summary), promoted attachment row + downloadable storage object, unselected file cleaned up with the staging folder, session confirmed with document_ids, updates-tab row unchanged, background AI pass produced a real summary + embedding chunks post-fix, attachment route 401s unauthenticated. All test records deleted. `tsc` + eslint + full build clean.
- **Noticed in ai_queries while testing:** Richard's "Colton" research run TODAY succeeded against tuaone@ (mailbox reconnect worked) but **info@ and moose@ were access-denied** — the grant is missing shared-mailbox access (`Mail.Read.Shared` consent and/or M365 delegation). Reports currently carry a per-mailbox skip note. Note the run staged with the OLD code, so that session has no attachments — the next run will.
- **FOLLOW-UP same day — local AI is now UNBUDGETED (Richard's call: the model is free, let it work as long as it wants):** `localChat`/`localChatStream` no longer send `max_tokens` at all; the `maxTokens` option is accepted but ignored in local mode (kept so callers share one shape with the Gemini path, where it still caps paid output). This permanently kills the empty-summary failure class. Verified live: unbudgeted call returns normally.
- **Shared-mailbox diagnosis (verified live):** tuaone's stored grant now DOES carry `Mail.Read.Shared` (+ ReadWrite.Shared etc. — the Azure re-consent worked), and tuaone@ search returns real threads. **info@ and moose@ still 403 `ErrorAccessDenied / "Cannot find row based on condition"` — that is EXCHANGE-level mailbox delegation, not an Azure API permission.** Fix is in Microsoft 365 admin center → Users → (info@ / moose@) → Mail → Mailbox permissions → **Read and manage (Full Access)** → add tuaone@berwilson.com (propagation can take minutes to ~1h). Azure App-registration permission grants cannot fix this. Also noted: a stale May-era `email_tokens` row for info@ exists — unused (graph-search authenticates only as tuaone@), harmless.

**Done 2026-07-11 (system-health maintenance surface — live probes + self-service mailbox reconnect; DEPLOYED):**
- **Context: Richard asked for a way to see and fix routine breakage himself** (the immediate case: the tuaone@ password change revoked the Graph grant and every mailbox feature died silently — the old health page still showed the token as "connected"). Decision: no separate portal, and NO password entry (OAuth never stores the password — the fix is always re-running consent); instead `/settings/health` became the single maintenance surface with the fix next to each diagnosis.
- **New `src/lib/system-health.ts`** (server-only probes, never throw): `probeGraphConnection()` — a REAL token refresh against Microsoft (success rotates+stores tokens, doubling as keep-alive; failure translates AADSTS codes to plain English: 50173 password-changed, 700082 inactivity, 7000222/invalid_client secret-expired → "reconnect will NOT fix, rotate the secret"), `probeLmStudio()` (pings `/models`, verifies both configured model ids are served), `probeBackups()` (stats newest file under `~/Backups/berwilson`, fail >30h; warn-not-fail when dir missing = dev machine), `probeDisk()` (`fs.statfs('/')`, fail <15GB / warn <50GB), `mailboxLooksBroken(expires_at)` (cheap DB-only heuristic: expiry >30h past — healthy worst case is ~23h because the daily brief refreshes daily).
- **`/settings/health` rebuilt around the probes:** checks gained an `action` button — the Microsoft check renders **Reconnect Mailbox → `/api/email/oauth`** with instructions (sign in as tuaone@ with the CURRENT password) and the exact redirect URI to add in Azure if Microsoft rejects (computed from forwarded headers). New checks: Local AI Engine (LM Studio), Nightly Backup, Disk Space, and optional **Azure client secret expiry** via new env `MICROSOFT_SECRET_EXPIRES=YYYY-MM-DD` (warn <30d — when that secret expires even Reconnect fails, so it's tracked explicitly). All stale Vercel copy replaced with Studio/launchd instructions (`~/Library/Logs/berwilson/`). AI-pipeline check is provider-aware ("Local Qwen").
- **Dashboard alarm:** the morning read now leads with a critical AlertsBanner line ("Mailbox disconnected — … Reconnect from System Health") when `mailboxLooksBroken` — one cheap `email_tokens` read, no network on dashboard load. OAuth callback success page links back to /settings/health; its stale Vercel copy fixed too.
- **Verified live against the real broken state:** probes exercised standalone (repo lib via alias-loader): Graph probe returned the actual AADSTS50173 with the friendly translation; LM Studio ok (both models served); disk 53/245GB; backups correctly "missing" on the MacBook; dev renders of /settings/health (all 9 checks + Reconnect button) and /dashboard (banner present) against the Studio DB. `tsc` + eslint (survivors = the 2 documented dashboard purity misfires) + full build clean; deployed to the Studio. **Richard still needs to click Reconnect Mailbox on /settings/health (sign in as tuaone@) to actually restore calendar/email research** — and if Microsoft shows a redirect-URI error, add the callback URL to Azure first (the page shows the exact URL).
- **FIX (same day): Richard's first Reconnect click failed with AADSTS50011 — the app sent Microsoft `https://localhost:3000/...` as the redirect URI.** Root cause: behind `tailscale serve` the Host header is rewritten to the backend (localhost:3000), so `request.nextUrl.origin` is wrong for anything user-facing; the real host travels in `x-forwarded-host`. New `src/lib/utils/request-origin.ts` (`publicOrigin(headers)`) — used by BOTH oauth routes (authorize + callback must byte-match) and the health page. **Rule: never use `request.nextUrl.origin` for user-facing URLs (OAuth redirect URIs, links) — use `publicOrigin(request.headers)`.** Verified the authorize URL now carries the tailnet redirect URI (standalone run against real env; client_id matches the Azure app 9e2420e3). Remaining Azure-side step for Richard: register `https://richards-mac-studio.tail0e5306.ts.net/api/email/oauth/callback` on the app registration (Entra ID → App registrations → Authentication → Web), then Reconnect.

**Done 2026-07-11 (raise/investor detail crash fix; DEPLOYED):**
- **Both `/investors/raises/[id]` and `/investors/[id]` crashed at render** ("Failed to load investors" — the `/investors` error boundary): `isPastDate` was defined in `InvestorsClient.tsx` (`'use client'`) and imported by those two **server** pages — every export of a client module becomes a client reference in server code, and calling one throws (`Attempted to call isPastDate() from the server…`). Reproduced in dev, fixed by moving `isPastDate` to `src/lib/utils/investors.ts` (plain module); all three call sites import from there. **Rule: helpers shared between server pages and client components live in `src/lib`, never exported from a `'use client'` file** — `tsc`/build DON'T catch this, it only fails at request time. Swept all 108 client modules: the only other lowercase function exports (`toKey`/`parseKey` in `calendar.tsx`) are consumed exclusively by client code — clean.
- Raise page's empty Investors state made actionable: names the raise to pick in an investment's Raise field, with "Go to investors" / "New investor" buttons (previously described the path with no link).

**Done 2026-07-10 (raises — named capital raises with tranche schedules; DEPLOYED):**
- **New concept: `raises`** (migration `20260710000003_raises.sql`, **APPLIED to the Studio DB**, supabase-rest restarted) — the container a round lives in: name, target_kind company|project (+project_id, same check as investments), `target_amount`, status planned|open|closed, dates, notes, and **`tranches` jsonb** (`[{label, amount, target_date}]`). Context: the first raise is **$88M in tranches of 25/25/25/13**; subsequent raises will follow — without this, Raise 2's commitments would be indistinguishable from Raise 1's. Plus **`investments.raise_id`** (FK, on delete SET NULL — deleting a raise never deletes money records).
- **Tranches are TARGETS, not ledger rows (Richard confirmed the model):** investors commit to the *raise*; tranches fill **sequentially from cumulative money levels** (waterfall fill). `src/lib/investors/raises.ts` holds the math: `raiseLevels(investments)` collapses the money triple into cumulative levels — per deal `wired=funded`, `signed=max(committed,funded)`, `potential=max(indicated,signed)` (robust to entry style; raw sums kept for stat bands) — and `fillTranches()` slices those levels across the tranche windows. Also `parseTranches` (tolerant jsonb parse) + `parseRaiseFields` (shared API validation). If per-tranche investor earmarking is ever needed, add a tranche tag on investments then.
- **Dashboard surfaces:** `/investors` gains a **Capital Raises band** (card per raise: status badge, target, segmented tranche bar with funded/committed/potential fills + legend, money line, investor count; open/planned before closed) + a New Raise button. **`/investors/raises/[id]`** is the per-raise dashboard: money band (Target / Committed / Funded / **Potential with gap-to-target** — "$X uncovered even if all indications convert"), tranche fill table (amount/committed/funded/to-commit/target date), and the per-investor table (deal stage, instrument, triple, next step, totals footer). `/investors/raises/new` + `[id]/edit` = `RaiseForm` (tranche editor rows, running sum vs target delta). Shared bar: `RaiseTrancheBar` (server-renderable divs; emerald=funded, indigo=committed, amber=indicated). No tranche schedule → the bar treats the whole target as one tranche; raises are admin-only like the rest of `/investors` (`/api/raises` not in any allowlist).
- **Investment tagging:** InvestmentsSection gains a Raise picker (only when raises exist) + raise chip on rows; `parseInvestmentFields` gains `raise_id`; investor detail fetches raises + joins them.
- **Wiring:** `embedInvestorSnapshot` investment lines say `under raise "<name>"`; daily-brief Capital Raise block leads with **per-raise standing** (target, committed/funded/potential, which tranche is filling); agent `list_investors` returns `raises` standings (incl. per-tranche fill) + per-investor raise names, `query_investor` investments include the raise; agent prompt explains raises + sequential-fill semantics. API: `POST /api/raises`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/raises/[id]` (PATCH is partial; name backfilled for status-only changes).
- **Verified live end-to-end** (repo lib via alias-loader against the Studio stack, 18/18 checks): waterfall math on the exact 88M/25-25-25-13 shape, raise+investment round-trip, both page joins, snapshot embedding mentions the raise, delete → raise_id nulls + investment survives, cleanup cascades chunks. `database.ts` hand-extended (`raises` + `investments.raise_id`); `tsc` + eslint + full build clean; deployed. (Note: the Finder-duplicate `.next/types/* 2.ts` files that break `tsc` reappeared and were deleted again — they're build artifacts.)

**Done 2026-07-08 (documents open/view/download again — post-cutover storage repair):**
- **Every document download in the app had been broken since cutover — two stacked causes, both fixed.** (1) All three document surfaces (projects DocumentsTab, VendorDocuments, OpportunityDocuments) created signed URLs **browser-side with the anon key**; the self-hosted storage has **zero storage RLS policies** (they don't survive a data-only dump/restore), so every request died with "Object not found". (2) Even server-side signing would have 500'd: the final cutover restored `storage.objects` metadata from cloud (cloud version UUIDs) *after* the file re-sync had written disk files under locally-generated version UUIDs — all 56 migrated objects pointed at nonexistent disk paths (`ENOENT` on `/var/lib/storage/stub/stub/<bucket>/<name>/<version>`).
- **Code fix: signed URLs are now minted server-side** (admin client, behind auth + viewer role checks — consistent with §8's "middleware is the boundary"): new `GET /api/documents/[id]` and `GET /api/opportunities/documents/[id]` return `{url}` (300s expiry; `?download=1` adds attachment disposition). Shared client helpers in `src/lib/utils/document-links.ts` (`viewDocument`/`downloadDocument`; popup-blocker-safe pre-open). **Filenames are now clickable → PDFs/images/text open inline in a new tab; non-viewable types (docx…) fall back to download.** The Download icon forces a download. **Rule: never use browser-side `createSignedUrl` against this stack — it cannot work.**
- **Data fix on the Studio (one-time):** `storage.objects.version` realigned to the actual on-disk version for all 56 mismatched objects (deterministic — every object dir held exactly one file; audited 57/57 across documents/media/avatars, zero missing/orphans). Verified live: inline view + forced download + public media + a fresh same-day upload all HTTP 200. **RESTORE GOTCHA for the runbook: after any `storage.objects` restore, metadata `version` must match the on-disk file name — re-sync files AFTER restoring metadata, or realign versions like this pass did.**
- AI summaries were already stored/rendered per document (`ai_summary` under each row) — unchanged. `tsc` + eslint clean; deployed to the Studio and verified (new route 401s unauthenticated, health check 200).

Verified signup now returns `signup_disabled`; existing logins unaffected. **New users are added by admin invite only.**

Steps 1/3/4/5 remain.
- **New `src/lib/ai/local.ts`** — OpenAI-compatible client for LM Studio (plain `fetch`, no SDK; §11 single-provider rule intact): `localChat`, `localChatStream` (SSE parsing + tool-call delta accumulation), `localEmbedding` (truncate >768-dim vectors to 768 + L2 renormalize — MRL — so the pgvector schema is untouched), `extractPdfText` (via new dep `unpdf`), and a stateful `<think>`-tag stream filter (Qwen thinking variants) — filter unit-tested incl. tags split across chunks.
- **Branch points, all gated on `AI_PROVIDER=local` (unset = byte-identical Gemini behavior):** `callGemini`/`callGeminiWithFile` (PDF → local text extraction + text call; image → OpenAI vision content, needs a VL model loaded), `generateEmbedding`, `runAgent` (full agentic tool loop rebuilt in OpenAI format — `agentTools` were already plain JSON Schema; Gemini-format history converted; streaming preserved), `transcribePdfText` (local mode = direct extraction, no model pass), `researchQuery` (**blocked in local mode** with a clear error unless `LOCAL_ALLOW_WEB_RESEARCH=true` — web research/enrichment is inherently external; only the search query would leave). Health page requires `LOCAL_AI_BASE_URL` instead of `GEMINI_API_KEY` when local. `ai_queries` logging kept on all paths (refactored into shared `logAiQuery`).
- **Env (documented in `.env.local`, commented out):** `AI_PROVIDER=local`, `LOCAL_AI_BASE_URL=http://100.86.79.4:1234/v1`, `LOCAL_AI_MODEL` (must match LM Studio's model identifier), `LOCAL_EMBEDDING_MODEL`, `EMBEDDINGS_PROVIDER` (overrides; defaults to AI_PROVIDER — **CRITICAL: query embeddings must match stored chunk embeddings; flipping to local without the step-3 re-embed silently breaks retrieval. Staged cutover: set `EMBEDDINGS_PROVIDER=gemini` until the re-embed runs**), `LOCAL_ALLOW_WEB_RESEARCH`.
- **LIVE-TESTED 2026-07-07 — works.** The Studio came online; actual model is **`qwen/qwen3.6-35b-a3b`** (Qwen 3.6 35B A3B, a hybrid-reasoning MoE) with `text-embedding-qwen3-embedding-0.6b` alongside (36GB fits both). `.env.local` is configured live: `AI_PROVIDER=local` + `EMBEDDINGS_PROVIDER=gemini` (until the step-3 re-embed). Verified end-to-end from lib-level scripts against the real DB: (1) `runAgent` full loop — company context injected, `list_projects` tool call executed, grounded streamed answer, 12.7s, ~12k tokens in; (2) real extraction prompt — correct assignees/risks/confidence, valid JSON, **~48s** (the model spends ~3k reasoning tokens; generation itself is ~75 tok/s); (3) embeddings 768-dim unit-norm; (4) `ai_queries` logging captured it all with the local model id. **Latency expectation: flash-equivalent tasks run ~30-60s locally vs ~5-15s on Gemini — that's the price of local.** Tested `/no_think` (Qwen soft switch): no meaningful token/latency reduction on real extraction — deliberately NOT adding a knob (no demonstrated benefit). LM Studio separates reasoning into `reasoning_content`, so the `<think>` filter is defense-in-depth only.
- **`.env.local` now also holds the Supabase URL/anon/service-role keys** (pulled via `supabase projects api-keys` — Vercel marks them Sensitive so `vercel env pull` leaves them empty). Local `npm run dev` is fully functional for the first time; Richard can log in and drive the app in local-AI mode in the browser.
- `tsc` + eslint + full `next build` clean. PDF extraction verified with a real file; think-filter unit tests pass.

**Done 2026-07-05 (code review + cleanup pass):**

- **Type-safety:** removed 12 stale `as any` casts (ProjectForm ×9, project detail, vendor detail ×2) — the generated types have covered those columns (capture fields, `entities.category`) since the last gen-types run. ~23 `any` escapes remain (§7 count updated).

Also fixed: expression-as-statement in `MeetingPrepButton`, missing `onSuccess` dep in `CompanyProfileClient`'s cert form effect, unescaped apostrophe.
- **Reviewed clean (no changes needed):** cron auth (fails closed), middleware gating, `permissions.ts` allowlists, `admin.ts` actor stamping, rate limiter, `vercel.json` crons; no dangling references to removed modules. **Done 2026-07-03 (production-readiness pass: cron fixes, agent self-fetch refactor, system health):**
- **The daily brief never ran in production — three compounding failures, all fixed.** (1) `/api/cron/daily-brief` was never in `vercel.json`'s crons (only risk-scores was) — now scheduled at 12:30 UTC (6:30am SLC summer). (2) It wasn't on the middleware public allowlist, so even an external trigger bounced to the auth wall — added (it self-guards via CRON_SECRET). (3) `CRON_SECRET` was never set in Vercel, so risk-scores has been 401ing daily too — generated and set in all three envs. **Security fix:** both cron routes' auth was `authHeader !== 'Bearer ${undefined}'` when the secret was unset — a literal `Bearer undefined` header passed on a public route. Both now fail closed (`!process.env.CRON_SECRET || …`).
- **Agent self-fetch antipattern removed.** `draft_email`/`draft_agenda`/`draft_status_report` and `get_attention_items` fetched `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000'` — broken in prod (env var never set; and cookie-less self-fetches would have hit the auth middleware anyway). The attention engine moved to `src/lib/attention.ts` (`computeAttention()`); draft generation moved to `src/lib/ai/draft.ts` (`generateDraft()`). Routes (`api/attention`, `api/ai/draft`) are thin wrappers keeping auth/rate-limit; agent tools call the libs directly. **Rule: agent tools never fetch the app's own HTTP routes — extract shared logic into `src/lib` instead.**
- **New `/settings/health` (admin-only, sidebar System group + mobile More):** live checks on every load — daily-brief + risk-scores cron freshness (fail >36h), AI pipeline (last `ai_queries` call + 24h count), Microsoft Graph token (connected mailbox, flags a missing `Mail.Read.Shared` scope → tells you to reconnect), failed email-research runs (7d), and required-env presence. Server component, direct admin-client queries, card idiom. This is the "background work fails silently" alarm surface.
- **Hygiene verified:** `npm run gen-types` run — generated `database.ts` byte-identical to the committed hand-extended file (user-access reconciliation done). Vercel env audit: retired vars already gone; `SYSTEM_USER_ID` no longer referenced in code. `tsc` + eslint + full `next build` clean.

- **Email research runs are visible immediately.** `api/email-research/run` stages a `running` `email_intake_sessions` row up front (label = search term), so navigating away no longer means minutes of "nothing happened"; every failure path (and a new top-level catch) flips it to `failed` with the error stored in `extraction_result.error`; success updates the same row to `pending` (new `sessionId` input on `analyzeEmailReport` — update-in-place instead of insert). Status values `running`/`failed` are app-level only (plain-text column, no migration). UI: Recent list renders running rows (spinner, non-clickable) and failed rows (error text + dismiss ✕); `SessionsAutoRefresh` re-fetches every 15s while a run is live; the `[id]` review page guards running/failed sessions (auto-refreshing "still running" card / error card); stale `running` rows (>15 min, function cap is 5) display as failed. New `api/email-ingestion/sessions/[id]` PATCH = dismiss (blocked for confirmed); dismissed sessions are hidden from Recent.
